# Memoria Tecnica - Verdemeta

## 1. Resumen ejecutivo
Verdemeta es una aplicacion orientada al seguimiento nutricional y de habitos, con enfoque en alimentacion basada en plantas. El sistema permite registrar alimentos, calcular indicadores diarios y consultar informacion historica para apoyo a decisiones de salud y nutricion.

## 2. Problema que resuelve
El software atiende la necesidad de registrar y analizar consumo alimentario de forma estructurada, especialmente para usuarios que siguen un estilo de vida vegano o plant-based y requieren trazabilidad diaria de sus metas nutricionales.

## 3. Descripcion funcional
Funciones principales:
- Registro de alimentos consumidos.
- Gestion de base de datos de alimentos.
- Seguimiento de metricas/objetivos diarios.
- Pantallas de autenticacion y perfil.
- Notificaciones para recordatorios diarios.

## 4. Arquitectura y componentes
Implementaciones identificadas en el repositorio:
- Frontend web: `index.html`, `css/`, `js/`.
- Migracion Flutter: `flutter-migration/lib/` con capas por modulos (presentacion, dominio, datos y servicios).
- Persistencia: scripts SQL y capa de acceso a datos (`flutter-migration/database/`, `lib/database/`).

## 5. Tecnologias utilizadas
- Flutter / Dart.
- HTML, CSS y JavaScript.
- SQLite (segun estructura y scripts de base de datos).
- Android build tooling (Gradle/Kotlin configs dentro de `flutter-migration/android/`).

## 6. Alcance de la entrega
Se entrega codigo fuente y documentacion tecnica asociada a la version:
- Version: [VERSION]
- Fecha de corte: [FECHA]
- Branch de referencia: `tst`

## 7. Elementos de originalidad (completar)
Describir en detalle los aspectos tecnicos que consideras novedosos:
- [ASPECTO 1]
- [ASPECTO 2]
- [ASPECTO 3]

Nota: esta seccion debe redactarse con precision tecnica y revision legal, ya que sera clave en cualquier analisis de patentabilidad.

## 8. Limitaciones y dependencias
- El sistema puede depender de bibliotecas de terceros con sus propias licencias.
- La proteccion sobre codigo propio no transfiere derechos sobre componentes de terceros.

## 9. Evidencia de autoria y fecha
Adjuntar en anexos:
- Historial de versiones (commits, tags o bitacora de cambios).
- Constancias de fecha (sello digital/notarial/registro oficial).
- Identificacion de autores y porcentaje de contribucion (si aplica).

- [ ] Calcule y guarde SHA-256 del ZIP.

================================================================================
ARCHIVO: paquete_patente/generar_paquete_fuente.ps1
================================================================================

$ErrorActionPreference = "Stop"
$ProgressPreference = "SilentlyContinue"

$root = Split-Path -Parent $PSScriptRoot
$fecha = Get-Date -Format "yyyyMMdd"
$dest = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "VERDEMETA_CODIGO_FUENTE_$fecha.zip"
$hashFile = Join-Path $PSScriptRoot "VERDEMETA_CODIGO_FUENTE_$fecha.sha256.txt"

if (Test-Path $dest) {
    Remove-Item $dest -Force
}

$excludeDirPatterns = @(
    "flutter-migration/build/*",
    "flutter-migration/recovered_from_apk/*",
    "flutter-migration/.dart_tool/*",
    ".git/*",
    "paquete_patente/*"
)

$files = Get-ChildItem -Path $root -Recurse -File | Where-Object {
    $rel = $_.FullName.Substring($root.Length + 1).Replace('\\', '/')

    $isExcludedDir = $false
    foreach ($pattern in $excludeDirPatterns) {
        if ($rel -like $pattern) {
            $isExcludedDir = $true
            break
        }
    }

    $isGeneratedArtifact = $rel -match "/(generated|outputs|intermediates|tmp)/"
    $isBinaryArtifact = $rel -match "\.(apk|aab|iml|lock)$"

    -not $isExcludedDir -and -not $isGeneratedArtifact -and -not $isBinaryArtifact
}

if (-not $files -or $files.Count -eq 0) {
    throw "No se encontraron archivos fuente para comprimir."
}

Compress-Archive -Path $files.FullName -DestinationPath $dest -CompressionLevel Optimal
$hash = (Get-FileHash -Algorithm SHA256 $dest).Hash

"SHA256: $hash" | Out-File -FilePath $hashFile -Encoding ascii

Write-Output "ZIP generado: $dest"
Write-Output "SHA256: $hash"
Write-Output "Hash guardado en: $hashFile"

  { name:'galletas veganas', aliases:['vegan cookies'], emoji:'ðŸª', cal:450, prot:5, carb:65, fat:20, fiber:2, sugar:35, iron:2, calcium:15, b12:0, zinc:0.5 },
  // â”€â”€ BEBIDAS (PDF) â”€â”€
  { name:'jugo de frutas', aliases:['fruit juice','jugo natural'], emoji:'ðŸ§ƒ', cal:45, prot:0.7, carb:11, fat:0.2, fiber:0.2, sugar:9, iron:0.3, calcium:11, b12:0, zinc:0.1 },
  { name:'agua de coco', aliases:['coconut water'], emoji:'ðŸ¥¥', cal:19, prot:0.7, carb:3.7, fat:0.2, fiber:1.1, sugar:2.6, iron:0.3, calcium:24, b12:0, zinc:0.1 },
  { name:'tÃ©', aliases:['tea','infusiÃ³n'], emoji:'ðŸµ', cal:1, prot:0, carb:0.2, fat:0, fiber:0, sugar:0, iron:0.2, calcium:0, b12:0, zinc:0 },
  { name:'cafÃ© negro', aliases:['coffee','cafe'], emoji:'â˜•', cal:2, prot:0.3, carb:0, fat:0, fiber:0, sugar:0, iron:0.1, calcium:2, b12:0, zinc:0 },
  { name:'kombucha', aliases:[], emoji:'ðŸ«™', cal:13, prot:0.3, carb:3, fat:0, fiber:0, sugar:2.5, iron:0.1, calcium:6, b12:0, zinc:0.1 },

  { name:'vitamina B12 vegana', aliases:['vitamin b12','suplemento b12'], emoji:'ðŸ’Š', cal:0, prot:0, carb:0, fat:0, fiber:0, sugar:0, iron:0, calcium:0, b12:500, zinc:0 },
  { name:'DHA/EPA de algas', aliases:['algae omega3','omega 3 vegano'], emoji:'ðŸŒŠ', cal:10, prot:0, carb:0, fat:1, fiber:0, sugar:0, iron:0, calcium:0, b12:0, zinc:0 },
];

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  BODY COMPOSITION
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function toggleHipField(){
  const g = document.getElementById('ob-gender').value;
  const hg = document.getElementById('hip-group');
  if(hg) hg.style.opacity = g === 'male' ? '0.4' : '1';
}

function liveBodyComp(){
  const weight = +document.getElementById('ob-weight').value;
  const height = +document.getElementById('ob-height').value;
  const waist  = +document.getElementById('ob-waist').value;
  const neck   = +document.getElementById('ob-neck').value;
  const hip    = +document.getElementById('ob-hip').value;
  const gender = document.getElementById('ob-gender').value;
  const age    = +document.getElementById('ob-age').value;
  const arm    = +document.getElementById('ob-arm').value   || 0;
  const thigh  = +document.getElementById('ob-thigh').value || 0;
  const calf   = +document.getElementById('ob-calf').value  || 0;
  const result = document.getElementById('bodycomp-result');

  if (!weight || !height || !waist || !neck || !gender) { result.style.display='none'; return; }
  if (gender === 'female' && !hip) { result.style.display='none'; return; }

  const comp = estimateBodyComp(weight, height, waist, neck, hip, gender, age, arm, thigh, calf);
  if (!comp) { result.style.display='none'; return; }

  result.style.display = 'block';

  // â”€â”€ Fat & LBM â”€â”€
  document.getElementById('bc-fat-pct').textContent    = comp.fatPct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bc-muscle-pct').textContent = comp.musclePct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bc-lbm').textContent        = comp.lbm + ' kg';

  // â”€â”€ Muscle highlighted â”€â”€
  document.getElementById('bc-muscle-kg').textContent  = comp.muscleKg + ' kg';
  document.getElementById('bc-muscle-idx').textContent = comp.smi + ' kg/mÂ²';

  // SMI category with emoji
  const catColorMap = { 'Sarcopenia': 'var(--rose)', 'Normal': 'var(--amber)', 'Optimo': 'var(--green)' };
  const smiEl = document.getElementById('bc-muscle-cat');
  const catKey = comp.muscleCat.replace('Ã³','o');
  smiEl.textContent = comp.muscleCat;
  smiEl.style.color = comp.muscleCat === 'Sarcopenia' ? 'var(--rose)'
    : comp.muscleCat === 'Normal' ? 'var(--amber)' : 'var(--green)';

  // â”€â”€ Precision badge â”€â”€
  const confEl = document.getElementById('bc-muscle-confidence');
  const p = comp.precision;
  confEl.style.display = 'block';
  const badgeColors = {
    'ðŸ”µ': 'rgba(58,130,170,.1)', 'ðŸŸ¡': 'rgba(184,118,58,.1)', 'ðŸŸ¢': 'rgba(46,125,82,.1)'
  };
  const borderColors = {
    'ðŸ”µ': 'rgba(58,130,170,.3)', 'ðŸŸ¡': 'rgba(184,118,58,.3)', 'ðŸŸ¢': 'rgba(46,125,82,.3)'
  };
  confEl.style.background = badgeColors[p.icon] || 'rgba(58,130,170,.1)';
  confEl.style.border = '1px solid ' + (borderColors[p.icon] || 'rgba(58,130,170,.3)');
  confEl.style.color = 'var(--text-dim)';
  confEl.innerHTML = p.icon + ' <strong>' + p.label + '</strong> ' + p.error
    + '<br><span style="font-weight:400">' + p.desc + '</span>';

  // â”€â”€ Bone & water â”€â”€
  document.getElementById('bc-bone-kg').textContent    = comp.boneKg + ' kg';
  document.getElementById('bc-water-kg').textContent   = comp.waterLitres + ' L';
  document.getElementById('bc-water-pct').textContent  = comp.waterPct + '%';

  // Category label with emoji
  const catLabel = {
    'elite':     '\u26A1 Atleta de \u00E9lite',
    'atleta':    '\uD83C\uDFC3 Atleta',
    'en_forma':  '\u2705 En forma',
    'aceptable': '\u2696\uFE0F Aceptable',
    'sobrepeso': '\u26A0\uFE0F Sobrepeso graso'
  };
  document.getElementById('bc-category').textContent = catLabel[comp.category] || comp.category;

  // Bone detail
  document.getElementById('bc-bone-detail').innerHTML =
    'Est. ' + comp.boneKg + ' kg (' + comp.bonePct + '%)<br>' + comp.boneCat;

  // Fluid detail
  const waterLabel = {
    'normal':     '\u2705 Hidrataci\u00F3n normal',
    'bajo_rango': '\u26A0\uFE0F Por debajo del rango normal',
    'retencion':  '\uD83D\uDCA7 Posible retenci\u00F3n de l\u00EDquidos'
  };
  document.getElementById('bc-fluid-detail').innerHTML =
    comp.waterLitres + 'L totales (' + comp.waterPct + '%)<br>' +
    'IC: ' + comp.waterIC + 'L &middot; EC: ' + comp.waterEC + 'L<br>' +
    (waterLabel[comp.waterSt] || comp.waterSt);

  // Composition bar
  const waterBarPct = Math.min(20, comp.waterPct * 0.4);
  document.getElementById('bar-muscle').style.width = comp.musclePct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bar-fat').style.width    = comp.fatPct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bar-water').style.width  = waterBarPct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bar-bone').style.width   = comp.bonePct + '%';
  document.getElementById('bar-rest').style.width   = Math.max(0, 100 - comp.musclePct - comp.fatPct - waterBarPct - comp.bonePct) + '%';

  // Color coding
  const fatEl = document.getElementById('bc-fat-pct');
  fatEl.style.color = comp.fatPct < (gender==='female'?20:10) ? 'var(--sky)'
    : comp.fatPct < (gender==='female'?32:25) ? 'var(--green)'
    : comp.fatPct < (gender==='female'?38:30) ? 'var(--amber)'
    : 'var(--rose)';

  const waterEl = document.getElementById('bc-water-pct');
  const nwRange = gender === 'female' ? [45, 60] : [50, 65];
  waterEl.style.color = comp.waterPct < nwRange[0] ? 'var(--amber)'
    : comp.waterPct <= nwRange[1] ? 'var(--sky)'
    : 'var(--purple)';

  // Muscle kg color by SMI
  const mEl = document.getElementById('bc-muscle-kg');
  mEl.style.color = comp.muscleCat === 'Sarcopenia' ? 'var(--rose)'
    : comp.muscleCat === 'Normal' ? 'var(--amber)'
    : 'var(--green)';
}

function estimateBodyComp(weight, height, waist, neck, hip, gender, age, arm, thigh, calf) {
  // â”€â”€ Input validation â”€â”€
  if (!weight || !height || !waist || !neck) return null;
  if (weight <= 0 || height <= 0 || waist <= 0 || neck <= 0) return null;

  const safeAge = (age && age > 0 && isFinite(age)) ? age : 25;
  const hM = height / 100; // height in meters
  const sex = gender === 'male' ? 1 : 0;

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  //  GRASA CORPORAL â€” US Navy formula (inputs in INCHES)
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  const toIn = cm => cm / 2.54;
  let fatPct;
  if (gender === 'female' && hip > 0) {
    const logArg = toIn(waist) + toIn(hip) - toIn(neck);
    if (logArg <= 0) return null;
    fatPct = 163.205 * Math.log10(logArg) - 97.684 * Math.log10(toIn(height)) - 78.387;
  } else {
    const logArg = toIn(waist) - toIn(neck);
    if (logArg <= 0) return null;
    fatPct = 86.010 * Math.log10(logArg) - 70.041 * Math.log10(toIn(height)) + 36.76;
  }
  if (!isFinite(fatPct) || isNaN(fatPct)) return null;
  fatPct = Math.max(3, Math.min(60, +fatPct.toFixed(1)));
  const fatKg = +(weight * fatPct / 100).toFixed(1);
  const lbm   = +(weight - fatKg).toFixed(1);

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  //  MASA MUSCULAR â€” Ensemble de 3 modelos
  //  Lee 2000 + Yang 2018 (pantorrilla) + Heymsfield 2011 (brazo)
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•

  // â”€â”€ Modelo 1: Lee et al. 2000 (RÂ²=0.86 vs BIA) â”€â”€
  // SMM = 0.244Ã—BW + 7.80Ã—Ht(m) + 6.6Ã—sex âˆ’ 0.098Ã—age + ethnicity âˆ’ 3.3
  // Calibrado Ã—0.85 para ajustar a referencia DEXA (Janssen 2000 n=268)
  const smm_lee = Math.max(5, (0.244*weight + 7.80*hM + 6.6*sex - 0.098*safeAge + 1.2 - 3.3) * 0.85);

  let smmModels = [smm_lee];
  let smmWeights = [1.0];

  // â”€â”€ Modelo 2: Yang et al. 2018 â€” pantorrilla (RÂ²â‰ˆ0.70) â”€â”€
  // ALM (appendicular lean mass) = f(calf circumference)
  // Validado en n=1680 adultos con DEXA
  const calfVal = +calf;
  if (calfVal && calfVal >= 20 && calfVal <= 58) {
    const refHcc = gender === 'male' ? 1.75 : 1.63;
    let alm_cc = gender === 'male' ? (0.65*calfVal - 4.3) : (0.52*calfVal - 2.1);
    alm_cc = Math.max(5, alm_cc);
    alm_cc *= Math.pow(hM / refHcc, 0.6);                        // height correction
    alm_cc *= Math.max(0.78, 1.0 - Math.max(0, safeAge-30)*0.004); // age decay
    const smm_cc = Math.max(5, alm_cc * 1.3); // ALM â†’ total SMM (+23% trunk)
    smmModels.push(smm_cc);
    smmWeights.push(1.8); // highest weight: pantorrilla is best single predictor
  }

  // â”€â”€ Modelo 3: Heymsfield 2011 â€” brazo (RÂ²â‰ˆ0.65) â”€â”€
  // MAC (mid-upper arm circumference) â†’ ALM
  const armVal = +arm;
  if (armVal && armVal >= 18 && armVal <= 55) {
    const refH_arm = gender === 'male' ? 1.75 : 1.63;
    const refMAC   = gender === 'male' ? 32.0 : 27.0;
    const baseALM  = gender === 'male' ? 32.0 * 0.55 : 27.0 * 0.48;
    const deltaALM = (armVal - refMAC) * (gender === 'male' ? 0.55 : 0.48);
    let alm_arm = Math.max(5, baseALM + deltaALM);
    alm_arm *= Math.pow(hM / refH_arm, 0.5);                       // height correction
    alm_arm *= Math.max(0.78, 1.0 - Math.max(0, safeAge-30)*0.004);
    const smm_arm = Math.max(5, alm_arm * 1.3);
    smmModels.push(smm_arm);
    smmWeights.push(1.5);
  }

  // â”€â”€ CorrecciÃ³n por muslo (Rolland 2003 / Yang 2018) â”€â”€
  const thighVal = +thigh;
  let thighCorr = 0;
  if (thighVal && thighVal >= 30 && thighVal <= 85) {
    const refThigh = gender === 'male' ? 56.0 : 54.0;
    const scale    = gender === 'male' ? 0.30 : 0.25; // kg ALM por cm thigh
    thighCorr = (thighVal - refThigh) * scale * 0.4;  // 40% weight (sin pliegues)
  }

  // â”€â”€ Weighted average â”€â”€
  const totalW = smmWeights.reduce((a,b) => a+b, 0);
  let muscleKg = smmModels.reduce((sum, v, i) => sum + v * smmWeights[i], 0) / totalW;
  muscleKg += thighCorr * 0.5; // apply thigh correction

  // Physiological bounds (% of body weight)
  const minSMM = weight * (gender === 'female' ? 0.20 : 0.25);
  const maxSMM = weight * (gender === 'male' ? 0.55 : 0.48);
  muscleKg = Math.max(minSMM, Math.min(maxSMM, muscleKg));
  muscleKg = +muscleKg.toFixed(1);
  const musclePct = +(muscleKg / weight * 100).toFixed(1);

  // â”€â”€ Precision badge â”€â”€
  const nModels = smmModels.length;
  const hasThigh = (thighVal && thighVal >= 30);
  const precisionMap = {
    1: { label: 'EstimaciÃ³n bÃ¡sica', icon: 'ðŸ”µ', error: 'Â±15%', desc: 'Agrega brazo y pantorrilla para mayor precisiÃ³n' },
    2: { label: 'EstimaciÃ³n moderada', icon: 'ðŸŸ¡', error: 'Â±9%',  desc: 'Agrega el otro perÃ­metro para mayor precisiÃ³n' },
    3: { label: 'EstimaciÃ³n avanzada', icon: 'ðŸŸ¢', error: 'Â±6%',  desc: 'Alta confianza â€” mÃºltiples medidas incluidas' }
  };
  const precision = precisionMap[Math.min(3, nModels)];

  // Skeletal Muscle Index (SMI) = SMM / heightÂ²
  const smi = +(muscleKg / (hM * hM)).toFixed(1);
  // SMI sarcopenia thresholds (Cruz-Jentoft 2019)
  let muscleCat = '';
  if (gender === 'male') {
    muscleCat = smi < 7.0 ? 'Sarcopenia' : smi < 8.5 ? 'Normal' : 'Ã“ptimo';
  } else {
    muscleCat = smi < 5.7 ? 'Sarcopenia' : smi < 6.8 ? 'Normal' : 'Ã“ptimo';
  }

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  //  MASA Ã“SEA â€” DEXA-validated percent-based model
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  const refH  = gender === 'female' ? 163 : 175;
  const boneK = gender === 'female' ? 0.038 : 0.045;
  let boneKg  = boneK * weight * Math.pow(height / refH, 0.3);
  if (safeAge > 40) boneKg *= Math.max(0.75, 1 - (safeAge - 40) * 0.01);
  boneKg        = +Math.max(1.5, Math.min(6, boneKg)).toFixed(2);
  const bonePct = +(boneKg / weight * 100).toFixed(1);
  const boneRef = gender === 'female' ? 2.8 : 3.8;
  const boneCat = boneKg >= boneRef * 1.1
    ? 'Densidad alta - Excelente'
    : boneKg >= boneRef * 0.9
    ? 'Densidad normal OK'
    : boneKg >= boneRef * 0.75
    ? 'Densidad baja - Aumentar calcio/vit D'
    : 'Densidad muy baja - Consultar medico';

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  //  AGUA CORPORAL â€” Watson 1980
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  let waterL = gender === 'female'
    ? -2.097 + 0.1069 * height + 0.2466 * weight
    : 2.447 - 0.09516 * safeAge + 0.1074 * height + 0.3362 * weight;
  waterL     = +Math.max(10, Math.min(80, waterL)).toFixed(1);
  const waterPct = +(waterL / weight * 100).toFixed(1);
  const waterIC  = +(waterL * 0.67).toFixed(1);
  const waterEC  = +(waterL * 0.33).toFixed(1);
  const nwRange  = gender === 'female' ? [45, 60] : [50, 65];
  const waterSt  = waterPct < nwRange[0] ? 'bajo_rango'
    : waterPct <= nwRange[1] ? 'normal' : 'retencion';

  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  //  CATEGORÃA DE GRASA â€” ACE ranges
  // â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
  let category = '';
  if (gender === 'female') {
    category = fatPct < 14 ? 'elite' : fatPct < 21 ? 'atleta' : fatPct < 25 ? 'en_forma' : fatPct < 32 ? 'aceptable' : 'sobrepeso';
  } else {
    category = fatPct < 6 ? 'elite' : fatPct < 14 ? 'atleta' : fatPct < 18 ? 'en_forma' : fatPct < 25 ? 'aceptable' : 'sobrepeso';
  }

  return {
    fatPct, fatKg, lbm,
    muscleKg, musclePct, smi, muscleCat, precision,
    boneKg, bonePct, boneCat,
    waterLitres: waterL, waterKg: waterL, waterPct, waterIC, waterEC, waterSt,
    category
  };
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  ONBOARDING
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function goStep(n){
  const name=document.getElementById('ob-name').value.trim();
  const age=document.getElementById('ob-age').value;
  const gender=document.getElementById('ob-gender').value;
  const weight=document.getElementById('ob-weight').value;
  const height=document.getElementById('ob-height').value;
  if(!name||!age||!gender||!weight||!height){showModal('âš ï¸ Campos requeridos','Por favor completa todos los campos obligatorios âœ¨');return;}
  document.querySelectorAll('.onboard-step').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('active'));
  document.getElementById('step'+n).classList.add('active');
}

function selectGoal(val, e){
  selectedGoal=val;
  document.querySelectorAll('.goal-card').forEach(c=>c.classList.remove('selected'));
  (e.currentTarget||e.target).classList.add('selected');
}

function finishOnboard(){
  if(!selectedGoal){showModal('âš ï¸ Objetivo requerido','Selecciona tu objetivo ðŸŽ¯');return;}
  const name=document.getElementById('ob-name').value.trim();
  const age=+document.getElementById('ob-age').value;
  const gender=document.getElementById('ob-gender').value;
  const weight=+document.getElementById('ob-weight').value;
  const height=+document.getElementById('ob-height').value;
  const waist=+document.getElementById('ob-waist').value||0;
  const hip=+document.getElementById('ob-hip').value||0;
  const neck=+document.getElementById('ob-neck').value||0;
  const thigh=+document.getElementById('ob-thigh').value||0;
  const arm  =+document.getElementById('ob-arm').value||0;
  const calfM=+document.getElementById('ob-calf').value||0;
  const activity=+document.getElementById('ob-activity').value;
  const bodyComp=(waist&&neck)?estimateBodyComp(weight,height,waist,neck,hip,gender,age,arm,thigh,calfM):null;
  let bmr=gender==='female'?10*weight+6.25*height-5*age-161:10*weight+6.25*height-5*age+5;
  const tdee=Math.round(bmr*activity);
  let calGoal=tdee, protMult=1.6;
  if(selectedGoal==='deficit'){calGoal=tdee-400;protMult=1.8;}
  if(selectedGoal==='gain'){calGoal=tdee+300;protMult=2.0;}
  const protGoal=Math.round(weight*protMult);
  const fatGoal=Math.round((calGoal*0.28)/9);
  const carbGoal=Math.round((calGoal-protGoal*4-fatGoal*9)/4);
  user={name,age,gender,weight,height,waist,hip,neck,thigh,arm,calf:calfM,activity,goal:selectedGoal,tdee,bmi:+(weight/((height/100)**2)).toFixed(1),bodyComp};
  macroTargets={calories:calGoal,protein:protGoal,carbs:carbGoal,fat:fatGoal,fiber:gender==='female'?25:38,sugar:Math.round(calGoal*0.05/4),iron:gender==='female'?18:8,calcium:1000,b12:2.4,zinc:gender==='female'?8:11,water:8};
  localStorage.setItem('vm_user',JSON.stringify(user));
  localStorage.setItem('vm_targets',JSON.stringify(macroTargets));
  document.getElementById('screen-onboard').classList.remove('active');
  document.getElementById('screen-app').classList.add('active');
  initApp();
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  INIT
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function initApp(){
  foodLog=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vm_log')||'[]');
  waterToday=parseInt(localStorage.getItem('vm_water_'+todayKey())||'0');
  loadLearned();
  const initial=(user.name||'U')[0].toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('user-avatar-top').textContent=initial;
  document.getElementById('greeting-name').textContent=user.name;
  document.getElementById('today-date').textContent=new Date().toLocaleDateString('es-ES',{weekday:'long',year:'numeric',month:'long',day:'numeric'});
  buildWaterCups();
  buildQuickFoods();
  renderDashboard();
  renderHistory();
  renderProfile();
  buildCharts();
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  WATER
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function buildWaterCups(){
  const container=document.getElementById('water-cups');
  container.innerHTML='';
  for(let i=0;i<8;i++){
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.className='water-cup'+(i<waterToday?' filled':'');
    btn.textContent=i<waterToday?'ðŸ’§':'â—‹';
    btn.title=`Vaso ${i+1} de 8`;
    btn.setAttribute('aria-label',i<waterToday?`Vaso ${i+1} marcado`:`Marcar vaso ${i+1}`);
    btn.setAttribute('aria-pressed',(i<waterToday).toString());
    btn.type='button';
    btn.onclick=()=>toggleWater(i);
    container.appendChild(btn);
  }
  document.getElementById('water-label').textContent=`${waterToday} / 8 vasos (${(waterToday*0.25).toFixed(1)}L)`;
}

function toggleWater(idx){
  waterToday=idx<waterToday?idx:idx+1;
  localStorage.setItem('vm_water_'+todayKey(),waterToday);
  buildWaterCups();
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  QUICK FOODS
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function buildQuickFoods(){
  const grid=document.getElementById('quick-foods-grid');
  grid.innerHTML='';
  quickFoods.forEach(f=>{
    const btn=document.createElement('button');
    btn.className='quick-food-btn';
    btn.type='button';
    btn.innerHTML=`<div class="qf-icon">${f.emoji}</div><div class="qf-name">${f.name}</div><div class="qf-cal">${f.cal} kcal / ${f.per}</div>`;
    btn.onclick=()=>addQuickFood(f);
    grid.appendChild(btn);
  });
}

function addQuickFood(f){
  const meal=document.getElementById('meal-time').value;
  const entry={...f,meal,date:todayKey(),time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString('es-ES',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),qty:100,id:Date.now()};
  foodLog.push(entry);
  saveFoodLog();
  renderDashboard();
  renderHistory();
  updateCharts();
  showToast(`${f.emoji} ${f.name} agregado Â· +${f.cal} kcal`);
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  SEARCH
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function searchFoodLive(query){
  const dropdown=document.getElementById('search-dropdown');
  const q=query.toLowerCase().trim();
  selectedFoodEntry=null; aiEstimatedFood=null;
  hideEl('food-preview'); hideEl('food-not-found'); hideEl('ai-result'); hideEl('ai-loading');
  if(q.length<2){dropdown.style.display='none';return;}
  const matches=foodDatabase.filter(f=>f.name.includes(q)||(f.aliases||[]).some(a=>a.includes(q))).slice(0,7);
  if(matches.length===0){
    dropdown.style.display='none';
    if(q.length>2){
      showEl('food-not-found');
      const mq=document.getElementById('m-qty').value;
      if(mq) document.getElementById('m-qty-ai').value=mq;
    }
    return;
  }
  dropdown.style.display='block';
  dropdown.innerHTML=matches.map((f,i)=>`
    <div class="dropdown-item" onclick="selectFood(${foodDatabase.indexOf(f)})" role="option" aria-selected="false">
      <span style="font-size:1.3rem">${f.emoji}</span>
      <div><div class="di-name">${f.name}</div><div class="di-cal">${f.cal} kcal Â· ${f.prot}g prot Â· ${f.carb}g carb / 100g</div></div>
    </div>`).join('');
}

function selectFood(idx){
  const food=foodDatabase[idx];
  if(!food) return;
  selectedFoodEntry=food; aiEstimatedFood=null;
  document.getElementById('m-name').value=food.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+food.name.slice(1);
  document.getElementById('search-dropdown').style.display='none';
  hideEl('food-not-found'); hideEl('ai-result');
  updatePreview();
}

document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
  const dd=document.getElementById('search-dropdown');
  if(dd&&!dd.contains(e.target)&&e.target.id!=='m-name') dd.style.display='none';
});

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  AI ESTIMATION
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
async function estimateWithAI(){
  const foodName=document.getElementById('m-name').value.trim();
  const qty=+document.getElementById('m-qty-ai').value||+document.getElementById('m-qty').value||100;
  if(!foodName){showModal('âš ï¸ Nombre requerido','Primero escribe el nombre del alimento que deseas estimar.');return;}
  hideEl('food-not-found'); hideEl('ai-result');
  showEl('ai-loading');
  document.getElementById('ai-loading-food').textContent=foodName;
  try{
    const response=await fetch('https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages',{
      method:'POST',
      headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
      body:JSON.stringify({
        model:'claude-sonnet-4-20250514',
        max_tokens:1000,
        system:`Eres un nutricionista experto en alimentaciÃ³n vegana. Devuelve ÃšNICAMENTE JSON vÃ¡lido sin texto adicional ni markdown:
{"name":"nombre normalizado en espaÃ±ol","emoji":"emoji relevante","cal":nÃºmero,"prot":nÃºmero,"carb":nÃºmero,"fat":nÃºmero,"fiber":nÃºmero,"sugar":nÃºmero,"iron":nÃºmero,"calcium":nÃºmero,"b12":nÃºmero,"zinc":nÃºmero,"note":"breve nota mÃ¡x 80 chars"}
Todos los valores por 100g/ml. Si no es vegano, estima el equivalente vegano mÃ¡s cercano.`,
        messages:[{role:'user',content:`Alimento: "${foodName}" | ${qty}g/ml`}]
      })
    });
    const data=await response.json();
    const rawText=data.content?.[0]?.text||'';
    const parsed=JSON.parse(rawText.replace(/```json|```/g,'').trim());
    hideEl('ai-loading');
    if(!parsed.cal&&parsed.cal!==0) throw new Error('Respuesta incompleta');
    aiEstimatedFood={name:parsed.name||foodName,emoji:parsed.emoji||'ðŸ½ï¸',cal:+parsed.cal||0,prot:+parsed.prot||0,carb:+parsed.carb||0,fat:+parsed.fat||0,fiber:+parsed.fiber||0,sugar:+parsed.sugar||0,iron:+parsed.iron||0,calcium:+parsed.calcium||0,b12:+parsed.b12||0,zinc:+parsed.zinc||0,aliases:[],aiGenerated:true};
    const exists=foodDatabase.some(f=>f.name.toLowerCase()===aiEstimatedFood.name.toLowerCase());
    if(!exists){foodDatabase.push(aiEstimatedFood);saveLearned();}
    const ratio=qty/100;
    document.getElementById('ai-prev-emoji').textContent=aiEstimatedFood.emoji;
    document.getElementById('ai-prev-name').textContent=aiEstimatedFood.name;
    document.getElementById('ai-prev-qty').textContent=`${qty}g / ml`;
    document.getElementById('ai-prev-cal').textContent=Math.round(aiEstimatedFood.cal*ratio)+' kcal';
    document.getElementById('ai-result-note').textContent=parsed.note||'âœ… Estimado con IA y aprendido para bÃºsquedas futuras';
    const mc={prot:'#2e7d52',carb:'#b8763a',fat:'#7050a8',fiber:'#3a9988',sugar:'#b84d65',iron:'#a05a2a',calcium:'#3a82aa',b12:'#5aad5a',zinc:'#8b5ac0'};
    const md=[{key:'prot',label:'ProteÃ­na',unit:'g'},{key:'carb',label:'Carbos',unit:'g'},{key:'fat',label:'Grasas',unit:'g'},{key:'fiber',label:'Fibra',unit:'g'},{key:'sugar',label:'AzÃºcar',unit:'g'},{key:'iron',label:'Hierro',unit:'mg'},{key:'calcium',label:'Calcio',unit:'mg'},{key:'b12',label:'B12',unit:'mcg'},{key:'zinc',label:'Zinc',unit:'mg'}];
    document.getElementById('ai-prev-macros').innerHTML=md.map(m=>`<div style="background:white;border-radius:10px;padding:8px;text-align:center;border:1px solid var(--border)"><div style="font-size:.82rem;font-weight:700;color:${mc[m.key]}">${+(aiEstimatedFood[m.key]*ratio).toFixed(1)}<span style="font-size:.62rem;color:var(--text-dim)"> ${m.unit}</span></div><div style="font-size:.65rem;color:var(--text-dim);margin-top:1px">${m.label}</div></div>`).join('');
    showEl('ai-result');
    document.getElementById('m-qty').value=qty;
  }catch(err){
    hideEl('ai-loading');
    showEl('food-not-found');
    showModal('âš ï¸ Error al consultar IA','No se pudo obtener informaciÃ³n nutricional. Verifica tu conexiÃ³n e intenta de nuevo.');
    console.error('AI estimation error:',err);
  }
}

function saveLearned(){localStorage.setItem('vm_ai_foods',JSON.stringify(foodDatabase.filter(f=>f.aiGenerated)));}
function loadLearned(){
  try{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vm_ai_foods')||'[]');saved.forEach(f=>{if(!foodDatabase.some(d=>d.name.toLowerCase()===f.name.toLowerCase())) foodDatabase.push(f);});}catch(e){}
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  PREVIEW
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function updatePreview(){
  if(!selectedFoodEntry) return;
  const qty=+document.getElementById('m-qty').value||100, f=selectedFoodEntry, ratio=qty/100;
  showEl('food-preview');
  document.getElementById('prev-emoji').textContent=f.emoji;
  document.getElementById('prev-name').textContent=f.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+f.name.slice(1);
  document.getElementById('prev-qty-label').textContent=`${qty}g / ml`;
  document.getElementById('prev-cal').textContent=Math.round(f.cal*ratio)+' kcal';
  const macros=[
    {label:'ProteÃ­na',val:f.prot,unit:'g',color:'#2e7d52'},{label:'Carbohidratos',val:f.carb,unit:'g',color:'#b8763a'},
    {label:'Grasas',val:f.fat,unit:'g',color:'#7050a8'},{label:'Fibra',val:f.fiber,unit:'g',color:'#3a9988'},
    {label:'AzÃºcar',val:f.sugar,unit:'g',color:'#b84d65'},{label:'Hierro',val:f.iron,unit:'mg',color:'#a05a2a'},
    {label:'Calcio',val:f.calcium,unit:'mg',color:'#3a82aa'},{label:'B12',val:f.b12,unit:'mcg',color:'#5aad5a'},
    {label:'Zinc',val:f.zinc,unit:'mg',color:'#8b5ac0'},
  ];
  document.getElementById('prev-macros').innerHTML=macros.map(m=>`<div class="macro-chip"><div class="macro-chip-val" style="color:${m.color}">${+(m.val*ratio).toFixed(1)}<span style="font-size:.65rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-weight:400"> ${m.unit}</span></div><div class="macro-chip-label">${m.label}</div></div>`).join('');
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  ADD FOOD
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function addManualFood(){
  const food=selectedFoodEntry||aiEstimatedFood;
  if(!food){showModal('âš ï¸ Selecciona un alimento','Escribe el nombre del alimento, selecciÃ³nalo de la lista, o usa âœ¨ Estimar con IA.');return;}
  const qty=+document.getElementById('m-qty').value||+document.getElementById('m-qty-ai').value||100;
  const ratio=qty/100, meal=document.getElementById('meal-time').value;
  const entry={
    name:food.name.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+food.name.slice(1), emoji:food.emoji,
    cal:Math.round(food.cal*ratio), prot:+(food.prot*ratio).toFixed(1), carb:+(food.carb*ratio).toFixed(1),
    fat:+(food.fat*ratio).toFixed(1), fiber:+(food.fiber*ratio).toFixed(1), sugar:+(food.sugar*ratio).toFixed(1),
    iron:+(food.iron*ratio).toFixed(1), calcium:+(food.calcium*ratio).toFixed(1),
    b12:+(food.b12*ratio).toFixed(2), zinc:+(food.zinc*ratio).toFixed(1),
    qty, meal, date:todayKey(), time:new Date().toLocaleTimeString('es-ES',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}),
    id:Date.now(), aiGenerated:!!food.aiGenerated
  };
  foodLog.push(entry);
  saveFoodLog();
  selectedFoodEntry=null; aiEstimatedFood=null;
  document.getElementById('m-name').value='';
  document.getElementById('m-qty').value='';
  document.getElementById('m-qty-ai').value='';
  hideEl('food-preview'); hideEl('ai-result'); hideEl('food-not-found');
  renderDashboard(); renderHistory(); updateCharts();
  showToast(`${food.emoji} ${entry.name} agregado Â· ${entry.cal} kcal`);
}

function saveFoodLog(){localStorage.setItem('vm_log',JSON.stringify(foodLog));}

function deleteFood(id){
  const item=foodLog.find(f=>f.id===id);
  foodLog=foodLog.filter(f=>f.id!==id);
  saveFoodLog(); renderDashboard(); renderHistory(); updateCharts();
  if(item) showToast(`ðŸ—‘ï¸ ${item.name} eliminado`,'#b84d65');
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  TOTALS
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function getTodayFoods(){return foodLog.filter(f=>f.date===todayKey());}
function getTotals(foods){
  return foods.reduce((acc,f)=>{
    acc.cal+=f.cal||0; acc.prot+=f.prot||0; acc.carb+=f.carb||0; acc.fat+=f.fat||0;
    acc.fiber+=f.fiber||0; acc.sugar+=f.sugar||0; acc.iron+=f.iron||0;
    acc.calcium+=f.calcium||0; acc.b12+=f.b12||0; acc.zinc+=f.zinc||0;
    return acc;
  },{cal:0,prot:0,carb:0,fat:0,fiber:0,sugar:0,iron:0,calcium:0,b12:0,zinc:0});
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  DASHBOARD
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function renderDashboard(){
  const foods=getTodayFoods(), tot=getTotals(foods);
  document.getElementById('cal-consumed').textContent=Math.round(tot.cal);
  document.getElementById('cal-goal-disp').textContent=macroTargets.calories||2000;
  const remain=Math.max(0,(macroTargets.calories||2000)-tot.cal);
  document.getElementById('cal-remain').textContent=Math.round(remain);
  document.getElementById('cal-remain').style.color=remain===0?'var(--amber)':'var(--green)';
  const pct=Math.min(100,(tot.cal/(macroTargets.calories||2000))*100);
  const ringColor=pct>105?'#b84d65':pct>90?'#b8763a':'#2e7d52';
  if(charts.ring) charts.ring.destroy();
  charts.ring=new Chart(document.getElementById('calRing').getContext('2d'),{
    type:'doughnut',
    data:{datasets:[{data:[pct,100-pct],backgroundColor:[ringColor,'rgba(46,125,82,0.12)'],borderWidth:0}]},
    options:{cutout:'75%',plugins:{legend:{display:false}},animation:{animateRotate:true}}
  });
  const macrosDef=[
    {key:'prot',label:'ProteÃ­na',color:'#2e7d52',unit:'g',target:macroTargets.protein},
    {key:'carb',label:'Carbohidratos',color:'#b8763a',unit:'g',target:macroTargets.carbs},
    {key:'fat',label:'Grasas',color:'#7050a8',unit:'g',target:macroTargets.fat},
    {key:'fiber',label:'Fibra',color:'#3a9988',unit:'g',target:macroTargets.fiber},
    {key:'iron',label:'Hierro',color:'#a05a2a',unit:'mg',target:macroTargets.iron},
    {key:'calcium',label:'Calcio',color:'#3a82aa',unit:'mg',target:macroTargets.calcium},
    {key:'b12',label:'Vitamina B12',color:'#5aad5a',unit:'mcg',target:macroTargets.b12},
    {key:'zinc',label:'Zinc',color:'#8b5ac0',unit:'mg',target:macroTargets.zinc},
  ];
  document.getElementById('macro-bars-container').innerHTML=macrosDef.map(m=>{
    const v=+(tot[m.key]||0).toFixed(1), t=m.target||1, p=Math.min(100,(v/t)*100);
    const barColor=p>=100?'#5aad5a':p>=70?m.color:'#b84d65';
    return `<div class="macro-bar-item"><div class="macro-bar-header"><span class="macro-bar-name" style="color:${m.color}">${m.label}</span><span class="macro-bar-val">${v} / ${t} ${m.unit}</span></div><div class="macro-bar-track"><div class="macro-bar-fill" style="width:${p}%;background:${barColor}"></div></div></div>`;
  }).join('');
  const container2=document.getElementById('foods-today');
  if(foods.length===0){
    container2.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state"><div class="empty-icon">ðŸ¥—</div>AÃºn no has registrado nada hoy.<br>Â¡Agrega tu primera comida!</div>';
  }else{
    container2.innerHTML=foods.map(f=>`<div class="food-item"><div class="food-emoji">${f.emoji}</div><div class="food-info"><div class="food-name">${f.name} <span style="font-size:.72rem;color:var(--text-dim);font-weight:400">${f.meal||''}</span></div><div class="food-macros">P: ${+(f.prot||0).toFixed(1)}g Â· C: ${+(f.carb||0).toFixed(1)}g Â· G: ${+(f.fat||0).toFixed(1)}g Â· Fibra: ${+(f.fiber||0).toFixed(1)}g</div></div><div class="food-cal">${Math.round(f.cal)} kcal</div><button class="food-del" onclick="deleteFood(${f.id})" title="Eliminar" aria-label="Eliminar ${f.name}">âœ•</button></div>`).join('');
  }
  renderTips(tot,macroTargets);
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  TIPS
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function renderTips(tot,targets){
  const tips=[], goal=user.goal||'health';
  const missing=[];
  if(tot.prot<targets.protein*0.7) missing.push('proteÃ­na');
  if(tot.fiber<targets.fiber*0.7) missing.push('fibra');
  if(tot.iron<targets.iron*0.7) missing.push('hierro');
  if(tot.b12<targets.b12*0.7) missing.push('vitamina B12');
  if(tot.calcium<targets.calcium*0.7) missing.push('calcio');
  if(waterToday<6) missing.push('agua');
  const goalTips={
    deficit:{consume:['Tofu, tempeh, legumbres como fuente proteica saciante','Vegetales de hoja verde en grandes volÃºmenes','Avena integral para carbos de liberaciÃ³n lenta'],avoid:['Aceites en exceso (aunque sean veganos)','AzÃºcares aÃ±adidos en batidos veganos','Snacks procesados veganos con muchas calorÃ­as']},
    gain:{consume:['Nueces y crema de manÃ­ como grasas saludables','Batidos de proteÃ­na vegana con plÃ¡tano','Quinoa y arroz integral para carbos de calidad'],avoid:['Saltarte comidas','Depender solo de vegetales bajos en calorÃ­as','Ignorar los carbohidratos complejos']},
    health:{consume:['Variedad de colores vegetales para antioxidantes','Semillas de chÃ­a y lino para omega-3','Alimentos fermentados como kimchi vegano'],avoid:['Ultraprocesados veganos','Exceso de azÃºcar aunque sea de frutas','MonotonÃ­a alimentaria']},
    maintain:{consume:['Balance entre legumbres, cereales y vegetales','Frutas frescas como snack principal','Agua como bebida principal siempre'],avoid:['Comer en exceso cuando cocinas grandes cantidades','Saltar el desayuno','Subestimar las grasas en recetas']}
  };
  const gt=goalTips[goal]||goalTips.health;
  if(missing.length>0) tips.push({type:'warn',icon:'âš ï¸',title:'Lo que te falta hoy',body:`<ul>${missing.map(m=>`<li>${m.charAt(0).toUpperCase()+m.slice(1)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`});
  tips.push({type:'good',icon:'âœ…',title:'DeberÃ­as consumir',body:`<ul>${gt.consume.map(c=>`<li>${c}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`});
  tips.push({type:'avoid',icon:'ðŸš«',title:'Mejor evitar',body:`<ul>${gt.avoid.map(c=>`<li>${c}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`});
  document.getElementById('tips-container').innerHTML=tips.map(t=>`<div class="tip-card ${t.type}"><div class="tip-head"><span class="tip-icon">${t.icon}</span><span class="tip-title">${t.title}</span></div><div class="tip-body">${t.body}</div></div>`).join('');
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  HISTORY
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function renderHistory(){
  const filter=document.getElementById('hist-filter')?.value||'today';
  const today=todayKey();
  const weekAgo=new Date(); weekAgo.setDate(weekAgo.getDate()-7);
  const wKey=weekAgo.toISOString().split('T')[0];
  let filtered=foodLog;
  if(filter==='today') filtered=foodLog.filter(f=>f.date===today);
  if(filter==='week') filtered=foodLog.filter(f=>f.date>=wKey);
  const tbody=document.getElementById('history-body');
  if(!tbody) return;
  tbody.innerHTML=[...filtered].reverse().map(f=>{
    const calPct=Math.round((f.cal/(macroTargets.calories/3))*100);
    const badge=calPct>120?'badge-rose':calPct>80?'badge-green':'badge-amber';
    return `<tr><td>${f.emoji} ${f.name}</td><td><span class="badge badge-green">${f.meal||'â€”'}</span></td><td><span class="badge ${badge}">${Math.round(f.cal)} kcal</span></td><td>${+(f.prot||0).toFixed(1)}g</td><td>${+(f.carb||0).toFixed(1)}g</td><td>${+(f.fat||0).toFixed(1)}g</td><td style="color:var(--text-dim);font-size:.78rem">${f.date}</td><td><button class="food-del" onclick="deleteFood(${f.id})" title="Eliminar" aria-label="Eliminar registro">âœ•</button></td></tr>`;
  }).join('')||`<tr><td colspan="8" style="text-align:center;padding:24px;color:var(--text-dim)">Sin registros en este perÃ­odo</td></tr>`;
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  PROFILE
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function renderProfile(){
  const initial=(user.name||'U')[0].toUpperCase();
  document.getElementById('profile-avatar-big').textContent=initial;
  document.getElementById('profile-name-disp').textContent=user.name||'Usuario';
  const goalLabels={deficit:'ðŸ”¥ Perder peso',maintain:'âš–ï¸ Mantener peso',gain:'ðŸ’ª Ganar mÃºsculo',health:'ðŸŒ¿ Salud general'};
  document.getElementById('profile-goal-tag').innerHTML=`<span class="badge badge-green">${goalLabels[user.goal]||''}</span>`;
  const bmiLabel=user.bmi<18.5?'Bajo peso':user.bmi<25?'Normal âœ…':user.bmi<30?'Sobrepeso':'Obesidad';
  document.getElementById('profile-stats').innerHTML=[
    ['Edad',user.age+' aÃ±os'],['GÃ©nero',user.gender==='female'?'Mujer':user.gender==='male'?'Hombre':'Otro'],
    ['Peso',user.weight+' kg'],['Altura',user.height+' cm'],
    ['IMC',`${user.bmi} (${bmiLabel})`],['TDEE',user.tdee+' kcal/dÃ­a'],
    ['Cintura',user.waist?user.waist+' cm':'â€”'],['Cadera',user.hip?user.hip+' cm':'â€”'],['Cuello',user.neck?user.neck+' cm':'â€”'],
    ['Brazo',user.arm?user.arm+' cm':'â€”'],['Muslo',user.thigh?user.thigh+' cm':'â€”'],['Pantorrilla',user.calf?user.calf+' cm':'â€”'],
  ].map(([l,v])=>`<div class="stat-row"><span class="stat-label">${l}</span><span class="stat-val">${v}</span></div>`).join('');
  // â”€â”€ Body Composition section in profile â”€â”€
  if (user.bodyComp) {
    const bc = user.bodyComp;
    const muscleColor = bc.muscleCat === 'Sarcopenia' ? '#b84d65' : bc.muscleCat === 'Normal' ? '#b8763a' : '#2e7d52';
    const bcHTML = `
      <div style="margin-top:16px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid var(--border)">
        <div style="font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--green);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:1px;margin-bottom:12px">ðŸ§¬ ComposiciÃ³n corporal estimada</div>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px">
          <div class="bc-metric"><div class="bc-val" style="color:var(--rose)">${bc.fatPct}%</div><div class="bc-label">% Grasa</div></div>
          <div class="bc-metric" style="background:rgba(46,125,82,.08);border:1px solid rgba(46,125,82,.2);border-radius:10px"><div class="bc-val" style="color:${muscleColor}">${bc.muscleKg} kg</div><div class="bc-label">Masa<br>muscular</div></div>
          <div class="bc-metric"><div class="bc-val" style="color:var(--teal)">${bc.lbm} kg</div><div class="bc-label">Masa<br>magra</div></div>
        </div>
        <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px">
          <div class="bc-metric"><div class="bc-val" style="color:var(--purple)">${bc.smi} kg/mÂ²</div><div class="bc-label">SMI (Ã­ndice<br>muscular)</div></div>
          <div class="bc-metric"><div class="bc-val" style="color:${muscleColor};font-size:.85rem">${bc.muscleCat}</div><div class="bc-label">Estado<br>muscular</div></div>
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:.7rem;padding:8px 10px;border-radius:8px;background:${bc.precision.icon==='ðŸŸ¢'?'rgba(46,125,82,.07)':bc.precision.icon==='ðŸŸ¡'?'rgba(184,118,58,.07)':'rgba(58,130,170,.07)'};color:var(--text-dim);line-height:1.5">
          ${bc.precision.icon} ${bc.precision.label} ${bc.precision.error}
        </div>
      </div>`;
    const statsEl = document.getElementById('profile-stats');
    statsEl.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', bcHTML);
  }

  const targets=[{name:'CalorÃ­as',val:macroTargets.calories,unit:'kcal'},{name:'ProteÃ­na',val:macroTargets.protein,unit:'g'},{name:'Carbos',val:macroTargets.carbs,unit:'g'},{name:'Grasas',val:macroTargets.fat,unit:'g'},{name:'Fibra',val:macroTargets.fiber,unit:'g'},{name:'Hierro',val:macroTargets.iron,unit:'mg'},{name:'Calcio',val:macroTargets.calcium,unit:'mg'},{name:'B12',val:macroTargets.b12,unit:'mcg'},{name:'Zinc',val:macroTargets.zinc,unit:'mg'},{name:'Agua',val:macroTargets.water,unit:'vasos'}];
  document.getElementById('macro-targets-disp').innerHTML=targets.map(t=>`<div class="goal-target-item"><div class="goal-target-val">${t.val}</div><div class="goal-target-unit">${t.unit}</div><div class="goal-target-name">${t.name}</div></div>`).join('');
  const totalEntries=foodLog.length;
  const daysLogged=[...new Set(foodLog.map(f=>f.date))].length;
  const avgCal=daysLogged>0?Math.round(foodLog.reduce((a,f)=>a+f.cal,0)/daysLogged):0;
  // Calculate streak
  let streak=0;
  const today=todayKey();
  for(let i=0;i<365;i++){const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()-i);const dk=d.toISOString().split('T')[0];if(foodLog.some(f=>f.date===dk)){streak++;}else break;}
  document.getElementById('progress-summary').innerHTML=`Total de registros: <strong style="color:var(--green)">${totalEntries}</strong><br>DÃ­as con registro: <strong style="color:var(--green)">${daysLogged}</strong><br>Racha actual: <strong style="color:var(--green)">${streak} dÃ­a${streak!==1?'s':''}</strong> ðŸ”¥<br>Promedio de calorÃ­as: <strong style="color:var(--green)">${avgCal} kcal/dÃ­a</strong>`;
}

function confirmReset(){
  showModal('âš ï¸ Â¿Reiniciar app?','Se borrarÃ¡n todos tus datos, registros y perfil. Esta acciÃ³n no se puede deshacer.');
  const actions=document.querySelector('.modal-actions');
  actions.innerHTML=`<button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="resetApp()">SÃ­, reiniciar</button><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" onclick="closeModal()">Cancelar</button>`;
}

function resetApp(){
  ['vm_user','vm_targets','vm_log','vm_ai_foods'].forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k));
  const keys=Object.keys(localStorage).filter(k=>k.startsWith('vm_water_'));
  keys.forEach(k=>localStorage.removeItem(k));
  location.reload();
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  CHARTS
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function getLast7Days(){
  const days=[];
  for(let i=6;i>=0;i--){const d=new Date();d.setDate(d.getDate()-i);days.push(d.toISOString().split('T')[0]);}
  return days;
}

function buildCharts(){
  const days=getLast7Days();
  const labels=days.map(d=>new Date(d+'T12:00:00').toLocaleDateString('es-ES',{weekday:'short',day:'numeric'}));
  const calData=days.map(d=>Math.round(getTotals(foodLog.filter(f=>f.date===d)).cal));
  const protData=days.map(d=>+(getTotals(foodLog.filter(f=>f.date===d)).prot).toFixed(1));
  const todayFoods=getTodayFoods(), tot=getTotals(todayFoods);
  Chart.defaults.color='#517a62';
  const gridColor='rgba(46,125,82,0.08)', tickColor='#517a62';
  const axisStyle={grid:{color:gridColor},ticks:{color:tickColor}};

  if(charts.cal) charts.cal.destroy();
  charts.cal=new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-calories').getContext('2d'),{
    type:'bar',
    data:{labels,datasets:[
      {label:'CalorÃ­as',data:calData,backgroundColor:'rgba(46,125,82,0.55)',borderRadius:8,borderSkipped:false},
      {label:'Meta',data:days.map(()=>macroTargets.calories),type:'line',borderColor:'#b8763a',borderDash:[5,4],pointRadius:0,fill:false,tension:0}
    ]},
    options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:tickColor,font:{size:11}}}},scales:{x:axisStyle,y:axisStyle}}
  });

  if(charts.pie) charts.pie.destroy();
  charts.pie=new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-macros-pie').getContext('2d'),{
    type:'doughnut',
    data:{labels:['ProteÃ­na (g)','Carbohidratos (g)','Grasas (g)','Fibra (g)'],datasets:[{data:[+tot.prot.toFixed(1),+tot.carb.toFixed(1),+tot.fat.toFixed(1),+tot.fiber.toFixed(1)],backgroundColor:['#3a8f5c','#b8763a','#7050a8','#3a9988'],borderWidth:0}]},
    options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,cutout:'60%',plugins:{legend:{position:'right',labels:{color:tickColor,font:{size:11},padding:12}}}}
  });

  if(charts.prot) charts.prot.destroy();
  charts.prot=new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-protein').getContext('2d'),{
    type:'line',
    data:{labels,datasets:[
      {label:'ProteÃ­na (g)',data:protData,borderColor:'#2e7d52',backgroundColor:'rgba(46,125,82,0.1)',fill:true,tension:0.4,pointBackgroundColor:'#2e7d52',pointRadius:4},
      {label:'Meta',data:days.map(()=>macroTargets.protein),borderColor:'#b8763a',borderDash:[5,4],pointRadius:0,fill:false}
    ]},
    options:{responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{labels:{color:tickColor,font:{size:11}}}},scales:{x:axisStyle,y:axisStyle}}
  });

  if(charts.micro) charts.micro.destroy();
  const microNames=['Hierro','Calcio','B12','Zinc','Fibra'];
  const microVals=[
    Math.min(100,Math.round((tot.iron/macroTargets.iron)*100)),
    Math.min(100,Math.round((tot.calcium/macroTargets.calcium)*100)),
    Math.min(100,Math.round((tot.b12/macroTargets.b12)*100)),
    Math.min(100,Math.round((tot.zinc/macroTargets.zinc)*100)),
    Math.min(100,Math.round((tot.fiber/macroTargets.fiber)*100)),
  ];
  charts.micro=new Chart(document.getElementById('chart-micro').getContext('2d'),{
    type:'bar',
    data:{labels:microNames,datasets:[{label:'% cubierto',data:microVals,backgroundColor:microVals.map(v=>v>=70?'#3a8f5c':v>=40?'#b8763a':'#b84d65'),borderRadius:8,borderSkipped:false}]},
    options:{indexAxis:'y',responsive:true,maintainAspectRatio:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{x:{max:100,...axisStyle,ticks:{color:tickColor,callback:v=>v+'%'}},y:{grid:{display:false},ticks:{color:tickColor}}}}
  });
}

function updateCharts(){buildCharts();}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  NAV
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function showView(name){
  document.querySelectorAll('.view').forEach(v=>v.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.nav-tab').forEach(t=>{t.classList.remove('active');t.setAttribute('aria-selected','false');});
  document.getElementById('view-'+name).classList.add('active');
  const tab=document.getElementById('tab-'+name);
  tab.classList.add('active'); tab.setAttribute('aria-selected','true');
  if(name==='charts') buildCharts();
  if(name==='profile') renderProfile();
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
//  MODAL & TOAST
// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function showModal(title,body){
  document.getElementById('modal-title').textContent=title;
  document.getElementById('modal-body').textContent=body;
  document.querySelector('.modal-actions').innerHTML=`<button class="btn btn-primary btn-sm" onclick="closeModal()">Entendido</button>`;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.add('open');
}
function closeModal(e){
  if(e&&e.target!==document.getElementById('modal-overlay')) return;
  document.getElementById('modal-overlay').classList.remove('open');
}

function showToast(msg, bg='var(--green)'){
  const t=document.getElementById('toast');
  t.textContent=msg; t.style.background=bg; t.style.display='block'; t.className='toast';
  clearTimeout(toastTimer);
  toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>{t.className='toast hide';setTimeout(()=>{t.style.display='none';},300);},3000);
}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
function showEl(id){document.getElementById(id).style.display='block';}
function hideEl(id){document.getElementById(id).style.display='none';}

// â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•â•
window.addEventListener('load',()=>{
  const savedUser=localStorage.getItem('vm_user');
  if(savedUser){
    user=JSON.parse(savedUser);
    macroTargets=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('vm_targets')||'{}');
    document.getElementById('screen-onboard').classList.remove('active');
    document.getElementById('screen-app').classList.add('active');
    initApp();
  }
});
</script>
